# Starting politics of 500 BCE for the parallelism test copy. Deliberately restless: every country has a shut-out ideology with real support,
# so movements, coups and civil wars happen within ten years. democratic = citizen assemblies; oligarchic = councils of elders; theocratic = priest-kings; neutrality = kings;
# fascist = warrior-nationalist factions; communist = commoner and slave levelers.
